# M4 AH: 182 daily content packages

12 October 2026–11 April 2027. 182 eight-slide carousels, 182 Reel scripts and 182 Story sequences. Each scenario is illustrative. See the strategy for publication dependencies and the workbook for Core/Optional selection. Artwork and rendered videos are not included.

All artifact text on slides 2–8 is counted within the 40-word maximum. Cover artifact entries describe art direction, not additional cover text. SHORT/DENSE indicates relative visual weight. Read the whole story before selecting a ranked alternative cover; rewrite slide 2 if a new cover changes the promise.

## 2026-10-12 · Week 1, day 1 · Busy Isn’t Booked

Theme: Meet Keisha. Find the leaks. Awareness: Problem aware. Offer fit: Growth Foundation. Visual mode: Salon scene.

One-sentence argument: Fixing this specific step, rather than adding more activity, creates a clearer process.

Ranked cover options (editorial judgement): 1. Busy Isn’t Booked; 2. Fix This Gap; 3. Keep Enquiries Moving; 4. Clarity Before Growth; 5. Control The Handover; 6. Stop The Guessing; 7. Check Before Scaling; 8. Make Booking Clear

### Carousel

SLIDE 1 · OPEN · SHORT
HEADLINE: Busy Isn’t Booked
BODY: none
ARTIFACT: One real prop showing the bottleneck; no extra visible text.
VERDICT: none
SLIDE 2 · HOLD · DENSE
HEADLINE: This is the gap
BODY: Keisha’s illustrative salon faces this: a full saturday hides an empty tuesday.
ARTIFACT: CURRENT: the request or decision stalls.
VERDICT: A busy owner needs a clear next step.
SLIDE 3 · SPINE 1 · SHORT
HEADLINE: Locate the break
BODY: none
ARTIFACT: CHECK: A full Saturday hides an empty Tuesday.
VERDICT: The visible symptom names the process to inspect.
SLIDE 4 · SPINE 2 · DENSE
HEADLINE: What happens next
BODY: Without a clear correction, staff or clients must guess the next step.
ARTIFACT: CURRENT → uncertainty → extra work.
VERDICT: Fix the gap before adding more activity.
SLIDE 5 · SPINE 3 · SHORT
HEADLINE: Change this step
BODY: none
ARTIFACT: REVISED: Compare booked hours with available hours by day.
VERDICT: Use a concrete instruction staff can apply.
SLIDE 6 · SPINE 4 · DENSE
HEADLINE: Test the change
BODY: Compare the current and revised step using one test record.
ARTIFACT: CHECK: Compare booked hours with available hours by day.
VERDICT: Record what happened; do not assume a result.
SLIDE 7 · TURN · SHORT
HEADLINE: Control the next step
BODY: So you can protect client time.
ARTIFACT: OLD: react to the symptom. NEW: control this step.
VERDICT: The story was about a usable process.
SLIDE 8 · PAYOFF · DENSE
HEADLINE: Make it practical
BODY: A clearer process gives the enquiry or decision a defined next step.
ARTIFACT: ACTION: Check one enquiry journey today.
VERDICT: none

CAPTION: Afro hair salon owners: a full saturday hides an empty tuesday. The practical correction: compare booked hours with available hours by day. Keisha is an illustrative character; this is a process example, not a client result. Check one enquiry journey today. #M4AfroHair #AfroHairSalon #SalonBusiness

ALT TEXT DRAFT: Illustrative M4 AH carousel for salon owners. It shows the bottleneck: a full saturday hides an empty tuesday; the correction: compare booked hours with available hours by day; and a practical process check.

### Reel

0–3s: Busy Isn’t Booked.
3–8s: Show the illustrative current step: A full Saturday hides an empty Tuesday.
8–16s: Show the revised step: Compare booked hours with available hours by day.
16–21s: Compare current and revised on a split screen.
21–25s: Check one enquiry journey today.
Voiceover: “A full Saturday hides an empty Tuesday. Try this: compare booked hours with available hours by day. A clearer next step gives the process an owner. Check one enquiry journey today.”
On-screen beats: “Busy Isn’t Booked” / “Find the gap” / “Change the step” / “Test it once”. Keep “Illustrative demonstration” visible. No claimed performance result.

### Story sequence

FRAME 1: “Busy Isn’t Booked” with the relevant prop. Label: “Illustrative example”.
FRAME 2: “A full Saturday hides an empty Tuesday.” Then show: “Compare booked hours with available hours by day.”
FRAME 3: Poll: “Is this step clear in your salon?” Options: “Clear” / “Needs work”. This poll is the only action; no second link or DM request.

### Optional commercial replacement

Destination: On-post process check. Keep the practical on-post action.
